#### Day 464: Send Bus Mixer Strips & Send Effects Rack Full Implementation (2026-05-10)
- **Feature**: Replaced the stub `openSendEffectsWindow` with a full implementation and added send bus mixer strips to the mixer
- **Files Modified**:
  - `js/ui.js`: Replaced stub `openSendEffectsWindow` with full implementation; added send bus mixer strips
  - `js/constants.js`: Bumped APP_VERSION to 2.136.0
- **Feature Details**:
  - **Send Effects Rack Window (full implementation)**: The window now has a proper effects rack UI with effects list, add-effect button, and parameter controls — mirroring the track effects rack pattern
    - `buildSendEffectsRackDOM()`: Creates the rack HTML with effects list container, add-effect button, and controls container
    - `renderSendEffectsList()`: Populates the effects list with up/down/reorder/remove buttons; wired to `addEffectToSendBus`, `removeEffectFromSendBus`, `reorderEffectInSendBus`, `setSendTrackEffects`, and `captureStateForUndo`
    - `renderSendEffectControls()`: Shows range/select/toggle parameter controls; wired to `updateSendBusEffectParam` and `setSendTrackEffects` with undo capture
    - `showSendAddEffectModal()`: Lists available effects from `effectsRegistryAccess.AVAILABLE_EFFECTS`; on selection calls both `addEffectToSendBus` (audio) and `setSendTrackEffects` (state) with undo capture
  - **Send Bus Mixer Strips**: Added send bus strips to the mixer after the track loop:
    - Each send shows: color dot, name, volume knob (wired to `setSendTrackLevel` + `setSendBusLevel` with undo), FX slot indicators, and FX button
    - FX slots are clickable and open the Send Effects rack for that bus
    - Context menu: Open Send Effects Rack, Mute, Remove Send Bus
  - **Add Send Bus Button**: Dashed '+ Send Bus' button at the end of the mixer creates a new send via `addSendTrack` (state) + `createSendBusInAudio` (audio), then refreshes the mixer
  - Both the Send Effects rack and the Add Effect modal keep audio and state in sync via `setSendTrackEffects`
  - Uses `effectsRegistryAccess` for `AVAILABLE_EFFECTS`, `getEffectParamDefinitions`, and `getEffectDefaultParams`
- **Verification**:
  - All JS files pass `node --check` syntax validation
  - ui.js, constants.js, and main.js all pass syntax checks

#### Day 461: Wire State Functions to appServices (2026-05-10)
- **Feature**: Added missing state function imports from `state.js` to `main.js` and exposed them on the `appServices` object for 8 feature groups that existed in state.js but were not accessible via the main service surface
- **Files Modified**:
  - `js/main.js`: Added imports for 56 state functions from `./state.js` and added 56 corresponding entries to `appServices`
  - `js/constants.js`: Updated APP_VERSION comment to reflect Day 461
- **Feature Details**:
  - **Track Groups** (10 functions): `getTrackGroups`, `getTrackGroupById`, `addTrackGroup`, `setTrackGroupName`, `setTrackGroupColor`, `addTrackToGroup`, `removeTrackFromGroup`, `setTrackGroupMuted`, `setTrackGroupSoloed`, `removeTrackGroup` — state management for organizing tracks into groups
  - **Track Templates** (6 functions): `getTrackTemplates`, `getTrackTemplateById`, `addTrackTemplate`, `updateTrackTemplate`, `removeTrackTemplate`, `clearTrackTemplates` — state management for saved track configurations
  - **Scale Mode** (10 functions): `getScaleMode`, `setScaleMode`, `getScaleModeEnabled`, `setScaleModeEnabled`, `getScaleModeScale`, `setScaleModeScale`, `getScaleModeRoot`, `setScaleModeRoot`, `getScaleModeLock`, `setScaleModeLock` — scale key, root, enabled, and lock state
  - **Ghost Track** (2 functions): `getGhostTrackId`, `setGhostTrackId` — show notes from other tracks in sequencer
  - **Loop Region** (8 functions): `getLoopRegion`, `setLoopRegion`, `getLoopRegionEnabled`, `setLoopRegionEnabled`, `getLoopRegionStartBar`, `setLoopRegionStartBar`, `getLoopRegionEndBar`, `setLoopRegionEndBar` — loop playback region state
  - **Swing** (6 functions): `getSwing`, `setSwing`, `getSwingEnabled`, `setSwingEnabled`, `getSwingAmount`, `setSwingAmount` — swing amount and enabled state
  - **Timeline Markers** (6 functions): `getTimelineMarkers`, `getTimelineMarkerById`, `addTimelineMarker`, `setTimelineMarker`, `removeTimelineMarker`, `clearTimelineMarkers` — timeline marker state
  - **Chord Mode** (10 functions): `getChordMode`, `setChordMode`, `getChordModeEnabled`, `setChordModeEnabled`, `getChordModeRoot`, `setChordModeRoot`, `getChordModeType`, `setChordModeType`, `getChordModeLock`, `setChordModeLock` — chord mode state with voicing, root, type, lock
  - These 56 functions existed in `state.js` with full implementation and undo capture but were never imported into `main.js` or exposed via `appServices`
  - Without this wiring, windows like Track Templates and Send Effects could be opened from `ui.js`, but there was no way to actually manage those resources from the UI
  - This completes the state wiring for all major feature groups that were restored in Day 458
- **Verification**:
  - All JS files pass `node --check` syntax validation
  - main.js, state.js, ui.js, audio.js, constants.js, tests.js, eventHandlers.js, and Track.js all pass syntax checks

#### Day 459: Wire Send Bus Audio Functions to appServices (2026-05-10)
- **Feature**: Added missing Send Bus Audio function imports from `audio.js` to `main.js` and exposed them on the `appServices` object so the UI can create/manage send bus audio nodes
- **Files Modified**:
  - `js/main.js`: Added import for 12 Send Bus Audio functions from `./audio.js` and added corresponding entries to the `appServices` surface
- **Feature Details**:
  - The following functions were imported from `audio.js` and added to `appServices`:
    - `createSendBusInAudio` (async) - Creates a Tone.js send bus with input gain, effects chain, and output gain
    - `deleteSendBusFromAudio` - Removes a send bus and disconnects all tracks from it
    - `getSendBusNodes` - Returns the Map of send bus nodes for UI display
    - `connectTrackToSendBus` - Connects a track's output to a send bus
    - `disconnectTrackFromSendBus` - Disconnects a track from a send bus
    - `addEffectToSendBus` - Adds an effect to a send bus's effects chain
    - `removeEffectFromSendBus` - Removes an effect from a send bus
    - `reorderEffectInSendBus` - Reorders effects in a send bus
    - `updateSendBusEffectParam` - Updates a parameter on a send bus effect
    - `setSendBusLevel` - Sets the output level of a send bus
    - `setSendBusMuted` - Mutes/unmutes a send bus
  - These audio engine functions are distinct from the Send Track state functions already exposed (which manage state/data)
  - The audio functions manage the actual Tone.js audio nodes that route audio from tracks to send buses to effects to output
  - Without this wiring, `addSendTrackState` could create a send track in state but there was no way to create the corresponding audio infrastructure
  - This completes the Send Bus audio wiring that was missing after the send track state was implemented
- **Verification**:
  - All JS files pass `node --check` syntax validation
  - state.js, ui.js, audio.js, and main.js are all syntactically valid
- **Version**: Bumped to 2.130.0

#### Day 453: Sequencer Step Probability UI (2026-05-07)
- **Feature**: Added step probability editing to the sequencer right-click context menu so users can set the chance that each step triggers during playback
- **Files Modified**:
  - `js/ui.js`: Added a new "Probability" section to the sequencer cell right-click context menu with preset values (100%, 75%, 50%, 25%, 10%) and a custom input option; updated the header line to show current probability; added helper functions `setProbability(row, col, prob)` and `promptProbability(row, col, currentProb)` that call `track.setStepProbability(row, col, probability)` and refresh the sequencer window
  - `js/tests.js`: Added a Day 453 test block covering `setStepProbability` and `getStepProbability` function exports, parameter counts, probability clamping to 0-1 range, stored probability retrieval, default value for non-existent notes, step data property storage, and APP_VERSION validation
  - `js/constants.js`: Bumped APP_VERSION to 2.127.0
- **Feature Details**:
  - The right-click context menu on any active sequencer cell now shows a "Probability" submenu with quick presets and a "Custom..." option that prompts for a 0-100 value
  - The menu header line now displays `Prob: X%` alongside Vel and Len
  - `setProbability` calls `track.setStepProbability(row, col, prob)` which captures undo state, clamps the value to 0-1, and stores it on `stepData.probability`
  - `getStepProbability` already existed and returns `stepData.probability ?? 1` — now it's accessible via the UI
  - Probability is already wired into the Tone.js playback callback (via `Math.random() < step.probability`), so setting it in the UI immediately affects playback behavior
  - Version bumped to 2.127.0

#### Day 452: MIDI CC Mapping Persistence (2026-05-07)
- **Feature**: Added project save/load support for MIDI CC mappings so learned controller assignments persist across sessions
- **Files Modified**:
  - `js/eventHandlers.js`: Added `getMidiCCMappingsForProject()` and `loadMidiCCMappingsFromProject(mappingsData)` to serialize/deserialize the runtime CC mapping table (`_midiCCMappings`)
  - `js/main.js`: Exposed the new functions on `appServices` and imported them from `eventHandlers.js`
  - `js/state.js`: Updated `gatherProjectDataInternal()` to include `midiCCMappings` in saved projects, and updated `reconstructDAWInternal()` to restore CC mappings after global settings
  - `js/tests.js`: Added a Day 452 test block covering the new persistence functions (exports, parameter counts, serialization format, deserialization with defaults, null/undefined handling, and APP_VERSION validation)
  - `js/constants.js`: Bumped APP_VERSION to 2.126.0
- **Feature Details**:
  - Runtime CC mappings (`_midiCCMappings` in eventHandlers.js) are now serialized as an array of plain objects when saving a project
  - When loading a project, the mappings are restored via `loadMidiCCMappingsFromProject()`, which clears existing mappings and repopulates from saved data with default fallbacks for missing fields
  - MIDI CC mappings (learned controller assignments) are now persisted with the same `.snug` project file — no extra setup needed
  - Version bumped to 2.126.0

#### Day 450: Recording Microphone E2E Track Resolver Tests (2026-05-06)

  - Returned results now include the selection source so callers can tell whether the resolver used an explicit, armed, or auto-selected track

  - Manual automation writes now record undo checkpoints, while volume knob gestures with automation armed still collapse into one clean history step

  - `js/constants.js`: Bumped APP_VERSION to 2.121.0
- **Feature Details**:
  - Sound browser navigation state is now isolated from accidental external mutation
  - Repeated writes of the same logical path no longer create redundant undo checkpoints
  - This makes the browser path state safer to pass around and keeps undo history cleaner
  - Version bumped to 2.121.0

#### Day 439: Track Undo Capture Coverage (2026-05-04)
- **Feature**: Expanded undo/redo capture coverage for remaining Track mutators that were still mutating without a checkpoint
- **Files Modified**:
  - `js/Track.js`: Added `_captureUndoState(...)` calls to:
    - `loadSampleToPad(padIndex, sampleSource)`
    - `addEffect(effectType)`
    - `removeEffect(effectId)`
    - `updateEffectParam(effectId, paramPath, value)`
    - `reorderEffect(effectId, newIndex)`
    - `setInstrumentSamplerLoopStart(time)`
    - `setInstrumentSamplerLoopEnd(time)`
    - `setInstrumentSamplerEnv(param, value)`
    - `setActiveSequence(sequenceId)`
    - `setNoteLength(row, col, lengthInSteps)`
  - `js/tests.js`: Added a Day 439 test block covering the new undo-capture coverage on Track mutators and the version bump
  - `js/constants.js`: Bumped APP_VERSION to 2.113.0
- **Feature Details**:
  - DrumSampler pad loading now records an undo checkpoint before persisting the new pad sample
  - Track effect edits now all participate in undo history, including add/remove/reorder and param updates
  - Instrument Sampler loop controls and envelope edits now participate in undo history
  - Active sequence changes and note-length edits now participate in undo history
  - This closes the remaining obvious undo-capture gaps in `Track.js`
  - Version bumped to 2.113.0

#### Day 438: Recording Microphone E2E Helper Hardening (2026-05-04)
- **Feature**: Hardened the recording microphone E2E helper so it selects a valid Audio track safely, rejects concurrent recordings, and returns a structured unsupported-browser failure when real microphone capture is unavailable
- **Files Modified**:
  - `js/audio.js`: Updated `runRecordingMicrophoneE2ETest(trackId, recordDurationMs = 2500)` to:
    - Prefer an explicit Audio track only when the requested track exists and is type `Audio`
    - Fall back to the armed Audio track or the first available Audio track
    - Return a structured `step: 'busy'` failure if recording is already active
    - Return a structured `step: 'unsupported'` failure if `navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia` is missing
    - Preserve the existing start/stop workflow, cleanup, and success/failure notifications
  - `js/tests.js`: Added a new Day 438 test block covering the helper export, parameter count, safe track selection, busy-state guard, unsupported-browser guard, and workflow references
  - `js/constants.js`: Bumped APP_VERSION to 2.112.0
- **Feature Details**:
  - The helper no longer assumes the requested track is the recording destination unless it is actually an Audio track
  - Real microphone testing now fails fast and clearly on browsers that cannot do live capture
  - Concurrent recording attempts are rejected before any new mic session is started
  - This keeps the E2E helper deterministic enough to use in the app without clobbering an active recording
  - Version bumped to 2.112.0

#### Day 428: DrumSampler Pad Drop Zone Verification Normalization (2026-05-04)
- **Feature**: Hardened DrumSampler pad drop-zone rendering so the selected pad index is normalized before use across the inspector, pad grid, and drop-zone setup
- **Files Modified**:
  - `js/ui.js`: Added a shared `getNormalizedDrumSamplerPadIndex(track, fallback = 0)` helper and used it to stabilize the selected pad container ID, pad highlighting, pad-number display, and drop-zone setup for DrumSampler tracks
  - `js/tests.js`: Added DrumSampler pad-drop-zone verification tests for the new helper and normalized selected-pad behavior
  - `js/constants.js`: Bumped APP_VERSION to 2.103.0
- **Feature Details**:
  - DrumSampler inspector no longer depends on raw `selectedDrumPadForEdit` values when building the active pad drop-zone container
  - Selected-pad display and pad grid highlighting now use the same normalized index, avoiding inconsistencies when the value is missing or malformed
  - The drop-zone setup path now uses the normalized pad index for both file input and drag-and-drop routing
  - Added helper coverage for empty-pad fallback and missing-vs-loaded metadata inference
  - This closes the pad drop-zone verification gap without changing existing sample-loading behavior

#### Day 412: Bug Fix - Missing Module Imports (2026-04-30)
- **Bug Fix**: Added missing `initializeUIModule` and `initializeAudioModule` imports to `main.js`
- **Root Cause**: `main.js` was calling `initializeUIModule(appServices)` and `initializeAudioModule(appServices)` without importing these functions from their respective modules
- **Files Modified**:
  - `js/main.js`: Added imports:
    - `initializeAudioModule` from `./audio.js`
    - `initializeUIModule` from `./ui.js`
- **Symptom**: "is not a function" errors during initialization, causing cascading failures (missing DOM elements, windows not opening)
- **Version**: Bumped to 2.90.0
